# Agents · P · part 1

946 entries, p-code-review-correctness to pattern-critic. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **p-code-review-correctness**: Invoked by p-code-review to run the correctness / code-quality channel across the entire working tree (no diff). Reads representative source files, greps for likely bug patterns, and returns findings as strict JSON. Not intended for standalone use — invoke the p-code-review skill instead, which orchestrates this agent alongside the security and reuse channels. ([Jayant2907/p-code-review](https://github.com/Jayant2907/p-code-review/blob/HEAD/agents/p-code-review-correctness.md))
- **p-code-review-reuse**: Invoked by p-code-review to run the reuse / duplication channel across the entire working tree (no diff). Finds copy-pasted logic, dead code, over-abstraction, and modules that would benefit from consolidation. Returns strict JSON. Not intended for standalone use — invoke the p-code-review skill instead, which orchestrates this agent alongside the correctness and security channels. ([Jayant2907/p-code-review](https://github.com/Jayant2907/p-code-review/blob/HEAD/agents/p-code-review-reuse.md))
- **p-code-review-security**: Invoked by p-code-review to run the security channel across the entire working tree (no diff). Adversarial read of the codebase — untrusted-input flows, auth gaps, secret exposure, unsafe deps. Returns strict JSON. Inherits the model selected in the invoking Claude session. Not intended for standalone use — invoke the p-code-review skill instead. ([Jayant2907/p-code-review](https://github.com/Jayant2907/p-code-review/blob/HEAD/agents/p-code-review-security.md))

- **p2e-architect**: Use when a supervisor executes a Standard- or Architectural-track story AND either the story's constraints include 'approach-review' OR the caller passed --full-team. Propose 2–3 approaches with trade-offs, recommend one, and sketch a 3–6-step implementation outline. No code edits. - **p2e-auditor**: P2E auditor subagent — holistic UXO/Flow review with per-AC AUDITOR assessments. MCP session is blind to verifier verdicts; uses criteria op=propose role=AUDITOR and op=list viewer_role=AUDITOR. ([columenlabs/p2e-plugin](https://github.com/columenlabs/p2e-plugin/blob/HEAD/docs/archive/agents/p2e-auditor.md))
- **p2e-reviewer**: P2E release reviewer — adversarial blind integration review of IN_REVIEW layers for a release batch. Use after verifier completes. Never reads verifier assessments; runs the app, re-tests evidence, proposes reviewer assessments via MCP. ([columenlabs/p2e-plugin](https://github.com/columenlabs/p2e-plugin/blob/HEAD/.cursor/agents/p2e-reviewer.md))
- **p2e-staff-engineer**: Use when a supervisor has N≥2 selected stories or batch orchestration needs a wave plan. Produce a wave dependency graph, estimate files touched per story, and flag same-wave file-collisions. No code edits. Output includes a JSON block the orchestrator parses. ([columenlabs/p2e-plugin](https://github.com/columenlabs/p2e-plugin/blob/HEAD/docs/archive/agents/p2e-staff-engineer.md))
- **p2e-story-lead**: Use when a supervisor dispatches one story for end-to-end implementation. Owns the per-story lifecycle — plan via the adaptive skill matrix, implement via nested workers, run the verify gate (verificationCmd + consumer sweep + adaptive fix loop), commit + PR at the risk-class-correct timing, run the tiered review tool — and returns a structured JSON report. Never writes story status; the supervisor owns all status flips. ([columenlabs/p2e-plugin](https://github.com/columenlabs/p2e-plugin/blob/HEAD/docs/archive/agents/p2e-story-lead.md))
- **p2e-verifier**: P2E verifier subagent — runs tests, captures evidence, and writes per-AC VERIFIER assessments via criteria op=propose. Dispatched after the coder story-lead finishes implementation. ([columenlabs/p2e-plugin](https://github.com/columenlabs/p2e-plugin/blob/HEAD/docs/archive/agents/p2e-verifier.md))

- **pack-inspector**: Use when debugging SRR pack-loading bugs — symptoms like `Cannot read properties of undefined (reading 'f')` from frontend nav.ts, the displayed article disagreeing with the URL hash filter, navigation skipping or crashing at a specific chronIdx, or any suspicion that idx pack bounds and data pack offsets disagree (often after a backend cron rewrite while a tab is still open). Runs `srr inspect` (e.g. `--from-hash '#2485!big_info'`) to verify the frontend's bounds-based (packId, offset) lookup against the actual data pack contents, against either a local packs dir or a live HTTP CDN URL. - **pack-matcher**: CGE Phase 2b — _project_profile.json + Discovery 결과로 packs/ 디렉토리의 어떤 도메인 팩을 활성화할지 점수 기반으로 결정한다. ≥80% 자동 권장, 50~80% 사용자 질의, &lt;50% 무권장. 사용자가 '팩 매칭', 'pack match', '도메인 팩 선택'을 언급하거나 cge-bootstrap Phase 2 진입 시 활성화. - **party-ba**: Party-mode panelist — problem fidelity. Attacks whether the proposal's stated problem is the real problem and whether its evidence is real. - **party-po**: Party-mode panelist — value per token. Attacks scope size, cost, the cheapest variant that captures most of the value, the do-nothing option, and the shipped defaults. - **party-security**: Party-mode panelist — trust boundaries and misfire behaviour. Attacks hostile input, fail-open failure modes, over-broad permissions, and irreversible runtime effects with no recovery path. Read-only; returns findings as its final message. Seated at tier deep or when a security domain flag is set. ([chan4lk/specclaw](https://github.com/chan4lk/specclaw/blob/HEAD/plugins/specclaw/agents/party-security.md) · ★12)

- **party-visionary**: Party-mode panelist — the long horizon. Read-only; returns findings as its final message. Seated at tier deep. ([chan4lk/specclaw](https://github.com/chan4lk/specclaw/blob/HEAD/plugins/specclaw/agents/party-visionary.md) · ★12)
- **pass-00-scoping**: Pass 0 of the elven-eyes audit workflow — Scoping & Repo Map. Builds the machine-readable codebase map every other pass consumes instead of re-discovering it. Runs first, alone, always, and produces no findings. Internal to the /elven-eyes command; not for standalone use. ([gguren/elven-eyes](https://github.com/gguren/elven-eyes/blob/HEAD/agents/00-scoping.md))
- **pass-01-injection**: Pass 1 of the elven-eyes audit workflow — Injection & Untrusted Input. Traces every external value from entry point to sink and flags paths reaching a sink without validation or context-appropriate encoding. Internal to the /elven-eyes command; not for standalone use. ([gguren/elven-eyes](https://github.com/gguren/elven-eyes/blob/HEAD/agents/01-injection.md))
- **pass-02-authentication**: Pass 2 of the elven-eyes audit workflow — Authentication. Audits how identity is established and maintained — session handling, credential verification, token lifecycle — never who may do what. Internal to the /elven-eyes command; not for standalone use. ([gguren/elven-eyes](https://github.com/gguren/elven-eyes/blob/HEAD/agents/02-authentication.md))
- **pass-03-authorization**: Pass 3 of the elven-eyes audit workflow — Authorization & IDOR. Assumes authentication works and audits only who may do what to which resource. Internal to the /elven-eyes command; not for standalone use. ([gguren/elven-eyes](https://github.com/gguren/elven-eyes/blob/HEAD/agents/03-authorization.md))
- **pass-04-secrets**: Pass 4 of the elven-eyes audit workflow — Secrets & Sensitive Data Exposure. Finds every place a secret or sensitive datum is exposed to someone who shouldn't see it. Internal to the /elven-eyes command; not for standalone use. ([gguren/elven-eyes](https://github.com/gguren/elven-eyes/blob/HEAD/agents/04-secrets.md))
- **pass-05-error-handling**: Pass 5 of the elven-eyes audit workflow — Error Handling. Verifies every failure path is handled explicitly and produces a state the caller can act on, with nothing silently swallowed. Internal to the /elven-eyes command; not for standalone use. ([gguren/elven-eyes](https://github.com/gguren/elven-eyes/blob/HEAD/agents/05-error-handling.md))
- **pass-06-concurrency**: Pass 6 of the elven-eyes audit workflow — Concurrency. Finds races, unsafe shared state, and interleavings that corrupt data under concurrent execution. Internal to the /elven-eyes command; not for standalone use. ([gguren/elven-eyes](https://github.com/gguren/elven-eyes/blob/HEAD/agents/06-concurrency.md))
- **pass-07-resource-lifecycle**: Pass 7 of the elven-eyes audit workflow — Resource Lifecycle. Proves every acquired resource is released on every exit path, including error paths. Internal to the /elven-eyes command; not for standalone use. ([gguren/elven-eyes](https://github.com/gguren/elven-eyes/blob/HEAD/agents/07-resource-lifecycle.md))
- **pass-08-data-access**: Pass 8 of the elven-eyes audit workflow — Data Access Patterns. Finds data-access patterns that are correct at small scale but become a bottleneck as data grows — N+1 queries, missing indexes, unbounded reads. Internal to the /elven-eyes command; not for standalone use. ([gguren/elven-eyes](https://github.com/gguren/elven-eyes/blob/HEAD/agents/08-data-access.md))
- **pass-09-complexity**: Pass 9 of the elven-eyes audit workflow — Complexity & Algorithmic Cost. Finds algorithmic complexity that will not survive the scale the system is expected to reach, with a stated breaking point for every finding. Internal to the /elven-eyes command; not for standalone use. ([gguren/elven-eyes](https://github.com/gguren/elven-eyes/blob/HEAD/agents/09-complexity.md))
- **pass-10-memory-growth**: Pass 10 of the elven-eyes audit workflow — Memory Growth. Finds unbounded memory growth over the life of a long-running process. Internal to the /elven-eyes command; not for standalone use. ([gguren/elven-eyes](https://github.com/gguren/elven-eyes/blob/HEAD/agents/10-memory-growth.md))
- **pass-11-external-calls**: Pass 11 of the elven-eyes audit workflow — External Calls, Timeouts & Resilience. Proves no single slow or failing dependency can take the whole service down. Also runs in Level 0 triage. Internal to the /elven-eyes command; not for standalone use. ([gguren/elven-eyes](https://github.com/gguren/elven-eyes/blob/HEAD/agents/11-external-calls.md))
- **pass-12-idempotency**: Pass 12 of the elven-eyes audit workflow — Idempotency. Determines whether operations that can be invoked more than once produce a duplicated side effect. Internal to the /elven-eyes command; not for standalone use. ([gguren/elven-eyes](https://github.com/gguren/elven-eyes/blob/HEAD/agents/12-idempotency.md))
- **pass-13-transactions**: Pass 13 of the elven-eyes audit workflow — Transactions. Verifies operations that must be atomic actually are, via a real transaction or an equivalent compensating mechanism. Internal to the /elven-eyes command; not for standalone use. ([gguren/elven-eyes](https://github.com/gguren/elven-eyes/blob/HEAD/agents/13-transactions.md))
- **pass-14-config-hardening**: Pass 14 of the elven-eyes audit workflow — Configuration & Environment Hardening. Finds environment/config-level exposure — debug surfaces reachable in prod, permissive defaults, under-hardened infrastructure. Internal to the /elven-eyes command; not for standalone use. ([gguren/elven-eyes](https://github.com/gguren/elven-eyes/blob/HEAD/agents/14-config-hardening.md))
- **pass-15-supply-chain**: Pass 15 of the elven-eyes audit workflow — Supply Chain & Dependencies. Judges whether the dependency and build pipeline can be trusted — pinning discipline, install-time risk, CI trust boundaries. Internal to the /elven-eyes command; not for standalone use. ([gguren/elven-eyes](https://github.com/gguren/elven-eyes/blob/HEAD/agents/15-supply-chain.md))
- **pass-16-logging-observability**: Pass 16 of the elven-eyes audit workflow — Logging & Observability. Verifies the system produces enough signal to diagnose an incident without leaking sensitive data or drowning in noise. Internal to the /elven-eyes command; not for standalone use. ([gguren/elven-eyes](https://github.com/gguren/elven-eyes/blob/HEAD/agents/16-logging-observability.md))
- **pass-17-api-contracts**: Pass 17 of the elven-eyes audit workflow — API Contracts. Verifies the API's actual behavior matches its declared contract so callers can rely on it without reading the implementation. Internal to the /elven-eyes command; not for standalone use. ([gguren/elven-eyes](https://github.com/gguren/elven-eyes/blob/HEAD/agents/17-api-contracts.md))
- **pass-18-cross-module**: Pass 18 of the elven-eyes audit workflow — Cross-Module Contracts. Verifies both sides of every module/service trust boundary agree on the contract; runs after the analysis passes, before Pass 20. Internal to the /elven-eyes command; not for standalone use. ([gguren/elven-eyes](https://github.com/gguren/elven-eyes/blob/HEAD/agents/18-cross-module.md))
- **pass-19-test-gaps**: Pass 19 of the elven-eyes audit workflow — Test Gaps. Finds where test coverage is absent or misleading for code that matters, not just a raw coverage percentage. Internal to the /elven-eyes command; not for standalone use. ([gguren/elven-eyes](https://github.com/gguren/elven-eyes/blob/HEAD/agents/19-test-gaps.md))
- **pass-20-verification**: Pass 20 of the elven-eyes audit workflow — Verification & False-Positive Filter. Relocates, falsifies, and conservatively re-scores every other pass's findings. Always runs last; no finding reaches a human without passing through it. Internal to the /elven-eyes command; not for standalone use. ([gguren/elven-eyes](https://github.com/gguren/elven-eyes/blob/HEAD/agents/20-verification.md))
- **pass-21-rate-limiting-abuse**: Pass 21 of the elven-eyes audit workflow — Rate Limiting & Abuse. Proves no entry point permits unbounded automated abuse — brute force, enumeration, resource exhaustion, business-logic abuse. Internal to the /elven-eyes command; not for standalone use. ([gguren/elven-eyes](https://github.com/gguren/elven-eyes/blob/HEAD/agents/21-rate-limiting-abuse.md))
- **pass-22-cryptography**: Pass 22 of the elven-eyes audit workflow — Cryptography. Finds every cryptographic primitive, key, or random value chosen or used incorrectly enough to break its guarantee. Internal to the /elven-eyes command; not for standalone use. ([gguren/elven-eyes](https://github.com/gguren/elven-eyes/blob/HEAD/agents/22-cryptography.md))
- **pass-23-migration-safety**: Pass 23 of the elven-eyes audit workflow — Schema Migration Safety. Verifies schema migrations are safe to run live — reversible or explicitly two-phase, with no unbounded write-blocking locks. Internal to the /elven-eyes command; not for standalone use. ([gguren/elven-eyes](https://github.com/gguren/elven-eyes/blob/HEAD/agents/23-migration-safety.md))
- **pass-24-compliance**: Pass 24 of the elven-eyes audit workflow — Compliance & Data Lifecycle. Verifies regulated data's full lifecycle — collection, storage, access, retention, erasure — is actually implementable end to end. Internal to the /elven-eyes command; not for standalone use. ([gguren/elven-eyes](https://github.com/gguren/elven-eyes/blob/HEAD/agents/24-compliance.md))
- **pass-25-ai-feature-security**: Pass 25 of the elven-eyes audit workflow — AI Feature Security (conditional, OWASP LLM Top 10 aligned). Runs only when Pass 0 detects LLM/agent features; otherwise writes a single skip line. Internal to the /elven-eyes command; not for standalone use. ([gguren/elven-eyes](https://github.com/gguren/elven-eyes/blob/HEAD/agents/25-ai-feature-security.md))
- **pass-engineer**: Compiler pass specialist for the lancy backend. Use when writing or reviewing analyses (CFG, liveness, dominance, reverse-post-order), transforms (SSA destruction, ABI lowering, legalization, pseudo cleanup, prologue/epilogue insertion), or when changing the pass pipeline ordering. Spawn proactively when a task involves pass ordering decisions, IR invariants, or cross-pass contracts. ([fexolm/lancy](https://github.com/fexolm/lancy/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/pass-engineer.md))
- **pass-prediction-engineer**: Implements deterministic ISS pass prediction, twilight and illumination gates, observer location handling, city lookup, and pass-card semantics. ([MertArtun/orbital](https://github.com/MertArtun/orbital/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/pass-prediction-engineer.md))
- **pass1-structural-rewriter**: You are Pass 1 of a text-to-speech rewrite pipeline. You do the judgment-heavy STRUCTURAL work only and leave the rest for a later pass. Read `rules_reference` first, in full — it defines your scope and what to leave untouched for Pass 2. Follow it exactly; do not step into token-level work. ([ArunskiOrg/BenArunskiUtils](https://github.com/ArunskiOrg/BenArunskiUtils/blob/HEAD/skills/tts/agents/pass1-structural-rewriter.md) · ★1)
- **pass2-token-sweep**: You are Pass 2 of a text-to-speech rewrite pipeline. Pass 1 already handled headings/numbering, the intro blurb, intra-document references, tables, and code stand-ins/segments. You apply EVERYTHING ELSE across the whole document, at the token level. Read `rules_reference` first, in full — it defines your scope and which of Pass 1's structural work to leave intact. ([ArunskiOrg/BenArunskiUtils](https://github.com/ArunskiOrg/BenArunskiUtils/blob/HEAD/skills/tts/agents/pass2-token-sweep.md) · ★1)
- **pass3-doubt-resolver**: You are Pass 3 of a text-to-speech rewrite pipeline, spawned only when Pass 2 reported significant doubts.
